| Reactive quality | 'PLYOMETRIC PURPOSE - BE REACTIVE & BOUNCY' (BA D22); '**PULSING/REP BOUNCING IS FOR TRANSITIONAL OR AMMORTIZATION PHASE' (BA C21). |
| Diagnosis by movement quality | Bands for 'SOMEBODY THAT CAN'T YIELD DOWN INTO A SQUAT CORRECTLY OR AT A "GOOD" SPEED' (SQ D36); 'SOMEBODY THAT IS SLOW ON THE WAY UP' (D38); 'SOMEBODY NEEDING TO STAY STIFF DURING THE WHOLE MOVEMENT ECCENTRIC, AMORTIZATION, AND CONCENTRIC' (D40). Bands are out of scope in v1 (EQ-01, EQ-12); the diagnosis is kept as record. |
| Range standard | 'Leg ext. Leg Curls (FULL RANGE OF MOTION)' (MM 07 L14); 'Side Raises FULL ROM' (MM 02 BB74); 'Elevated Push ups (for full ROM)' (LIB AD99). |
| Intent cues | '12/12 powerful turns' (MM 02 V30); 'Really high jump squats LOAD & jump hard' (MM 05 C14); 'Side to side shuffle floor slam (height matters)' (BA C80). |
| Skill progression | 'TEACHING/ LEARNING THE SKILL' / 'PROGRESSION MODEL EASY TO HARD' (OL A4:A5): high hang -> mid hang -> low hang -> floor; power before squat catch. |
| Client self-screen | 'Felt the RIGHT muscles' / 'Felt the WRONG muscle' / 'Felt a PAIN' / "Couldn't do it" (LIB AY8:AY11, header 'FEELING' AY1; program column 'HOW IT FELT?' JL J2); EFFORT '8 - That was perfect' (LIB AX15); coach note 'OUCH' (JL H12); round 2: '10' = "That was the max I can do, not a fail" (COACH-R2 VOL-02). |
| Pain rule | '****When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral or/and also getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going split grips (dumbbells, kbs, indivitual grips) help take care of some stuff' (MM 05 A6). |
| Effort model (coach answer) | VOL-13: 'I like RIR in my model, so that's what I'm going to be using. We can also add the explanation of RIR and kind of mix it with RPE... Everything in training is kind of subjective.' |

### 6.2 What the science adds (SCIENCE)

* Velocity loss inside a set shapes the adaptation [S39]: stopping at 20 % velocity loss gave similar squat strength gains to 40 % (no significant difference) and a better CMJ (+9.5 % vs +3.5 %) with 40 % fewer reps; 40 % gave more hypertrophy and shifted fibres away from type IIX.
* Intent produces velocity-specific adaptations even when the bar cannot move fast [S37]; maximal intended velocity nearly doubled bench 1RM gains vs half speed [S38].
* Proximity to failure matters for hypertrophy, little for strength [S21]; failure vs 1-2 RIR adds a trivial growth effect at a higher fatigue cost [S20]. The safe place for failure is a high-constraint machine or isolation (10b EQ-3).
* Free-weight injuries mostly involve dropped weights [S11]: technical failure on barbell lifts is the risk point.
* Plyometric quality: fast SSC contacts ~100-250 ms with small joint excursions [S57]; drop height is the intensity dial, ~30-40 cm optimal by RSI [S58][S59]; landing before reactive work matches 'TAKING IN FORCES' first.
* Heavier medballs make the head drift and the spine side-bend, which kills the hip contribution [S54].
* RIR scale: RPE 8 = 2 RIR, RPE 9 = 1 RIR (Zourdos 2016, via 03 5.3).

### 6.3 Generator rules (QM-x)

| ID | Rule | Status |
|---|---|---|
| QM-1 | Set termination by zone. Zones A-E (speed and power): stop at the first visibly slower rep or a form change (plyos 5-10 contacts, throws 5-10, loaded jumps 3-6; Olympic sets: DB/KB and pulls by the PER-09 ramp, full squat and power catches 3-5 reps, COACH-R2 ATH-05). Strength and hypertrophy compounds: 1-3 RIR. Small-muscle and bodyweight work: MAX (technical failure) on the last set allowed. MYO on the last machine/isolation exercise. Deload: 3+ RIR. A set that felt like a '10' is a successful max (0 RIR): the printed guide says take one set off that exercise next time (3.5; COACH-R3 VOL-02). | COACH-ANSWER VOL-13 (1-3 RIR), PER-09; COACH-R2 ATH-05, VOL-02 + OBSERVED effort cells + SCIENCE [S39][S20][S21] |
| QM-2 | A rep counts only if it meets the position and ROM standard (full ROM by default; 'MAXIMAL UPRIGHT POSTURE' on squats; full lockout and stretch on isolation). The first rep that misses the standard ends the set: that is technical failure. MAX only on small-muscle or bodyweight work (VOL-13); MYO only on the last machine or isolation exercise (VOL-15); never on 'the work' or a barbell main lift. | COACH-ANSWER VOL-13, VOL-15, CAT-04 + OBSERVED SQ A9; rule N13; definition INFERRED |
| QM-3 | Velocity-loss proxy without a device: cue 'same speed for all reps'. With a device: VL 10-20 % for power and speed, 20-30 % for strength, 30-40 % allowed for hypertrophy. | OBSERVED MM 02 BU33; SCIENCE [S39]; thresholds INFERRED |
| QM-4 | Quality gate before load goes up (printed for the client): add load (double progression) only when the last exposure felt the right muscles, at or under the target effort, through full ROM; otherwise hold the load. The client judges it; nothing is logged back (COACH-R3 VOL-02). | INFERRED from the BYOP dropdowns |
| QM-5 | Regression on failure to perform: "Couldn't do it" -> one level down in the same column or an assisted version (no bands: machine or bodyweight assistance), volume unchanged. 'Felt a PAIN' -> swap in category (split grip, unilateral, +1 constraint, one load-position step back, pain-free ROM) keeping the sets if possible; then -30-50 %; then drop the exercise (VOL-03, UNI-08). | COACH-ANSWER VOL-03, UNI-08, EQ-01 + OBSERVED MM 00 A7 / MM 05 A6; CAT-04 |
| QM-6 | Medball quality gate (LV-3): slower throw, head drift or spine side-bend -> 2 lb lighter. | COACH-ANSWER EQ-06 + SCIENCE [S54] + COACH-CHAT |
| QM-7 | Receive-Create stage gating (not 'zones', CAT-11; names and timing COACH-R2 ATH-03): 1 Land (receive & pause) -> 2 Land & Go only after two sessions of clean 3-s sticks (no knee collapse, quiet landing); -> 3 Repeat only after clean single responses; planned timing 12 weeks 1-3 / 4-7 / 8-12, 8 weeks 1-2 / 3-5 / 6-8, held longer when the gate is not met (INFERRED); restart at stage 1 each off-season, stay put in maintenance; intermediate and advanced athletes may start at stage 2; bilateral before unilateral inside each stage (unilateral is harder, CAT-10). A height or box change resets to the simpler pattern ('Bigger plates start at lateral bilateral plate hops'). Stop a set when contact time visibly lengthens or the heel drops. | COACH-ANSWER CAT-10, CAT-11, EQ-16; COACH-R2 ATH-03 + OBSERVED MM 05 F12, BA A24:D24, LIB G53; SCIENCE [S57]-[S59] |
| QM-8 | Olympic skill ladder: hang pulls before a power catch; high -> mid -> low hang -> floor; power before squat receive ('Squat Snatch', 'Squat Clean', LIB-07). Never to failure; strength plans follow the 75 -> 90 % ramp with 4 x 8 -> 4 x 4 (PER-09) and stop a set early when the bar slows; technique sets in deloads. Older or injured clients never snatch (EQ-03); older adults (60+) get no Olympic lifts by default, optional with a warning (COACH-R2 ATH-08); youth may lift from the floor from about 14, after one block of hang work (ATH-09; COACH-R2 ATH-08); DB/KB and pulls follow the ramp, full squat catches 3-5 reps with the same weekly reps, with the coach's eye on speed (COACH-R2 ATH-05). | COACH-ANSWER PER-09, LIB-07, EQ-03, ATH-09; COACH-R2 ATH-05, ATH-08 + OBSERVED OL A5:A30, LIB C tiers |
| QM-9 | Fatigue-sensitive work first. R3/R4/N5 exist so quality work is done fresh. Printed guide: an athlete whose previous session felt like a 9-10 keeps the athletic block but does one set less; after a heavy play or game day the client may switch the athletic block off for that day (COACH-R3 SES-02, ATH-05). | INFERRED from section 5; COACH-R3 SES-02 |
| QM-10 | Machines for quality under fatigue: if a client cannot keep position on a free-weight version late in a session or block, move that slot up one constraint level (or one load-position step back) rather than lowering effort. Older, injured and high-volume clients collect their volume on constrained machines (EQ-03). | COACH-ANSWER EQ-03, CAT-04 + COACH-CHAT; 10b EQ-1/EQ-5 |
| QM-11 | Upright posture is the foundational standard: a client who cannot hold 'MAXIMAL UPRIGHT POSTURE' in an on-the-body or back-loaded squat goes back to a front-loaded version (goblet, zercher, 'Low DB squats' with the DBs hanging at the sides) for the next block, whatever the client's level. | COACH-ANSWER CAT-04 ('people need to learn how to be more upright'), LIB-09 + OBSERVED SQ A9; rule INFERRED |

The V-squat is the exception: never offered, because 'no V-squat' is a GEN-08 hard rule (RIGHT), not a ranking. |
| CA-13 | Core position | ATH-11 NOT RIGHT: core first for everybody, movable | BYOP core only on 'Optional' days; BASEBALL core after the power block (Part B) | Core block first in every session; the user may move it. |
| CA-14 | Olympic reps | PER-09 RIGHT: 75 -> 90 % with 4 x 8 -> 4 x 6 -> 4 x 6 -> 4 x 4 in strength-focused plans | JL 'Power Clean' 2 x '6 to 8 Reps' (E7:G7); BASEBALL OLY rows | Settled by COACH-R2 ATH-05 (RIGHT): DB/KB and pull variants keep the 4 x 8 → 4 x 4 ramp; full squat cleans and snatches use 3-5 reps per set with the same weekly reps; barbell power catches follow the 3-5 rule (INFERRED; JL's 'Power Clean' 2 x '6 to 8 Reps' is not reproduced); % only when tested, otherwise RPE (COACH-R2 GEN-06). |
| CA-15 | Calves first and missing rear-delt / calf sets | ORD-04: first only when that muscle is a priority (v1.2: a Focus muscle); VOL-17: calves 3 sets every leg day, rear delts their own sets ('It wasn't intentional'); COACH-R3 SES-01, VOL-01: one calf exercise per leg day, rear delts inside the shoulders group | 'Calves' slot 1 on aesthetics A days (00 D3); about 3 indirect rear-delt sets a week in BYOP | Calves at the end unless a priority; calves on every leg day; a rear-delt station on pull days (Example A). |
| CA-16 | Deloads inside a plan | COACH-R2 VOL-07: maintenance or deload "should never be thrown in the middle of a block"; deload = half of the hardest week, between blocks | 4T STRENGTH-12 week 7 'MID DELOAD' (RPE 7); AESTHETICS-12 week 7 (3 sets); BOXING week 7 (eleven rows 3 → 2 sets); 03 9.8 | Not reproduced: no deload inside a block; v1.1's holds, -1 set after a "10", repeated weeks and block-at-risk prompt are retired with the check-in (COACH-R3 VOL-02); the printed self-adjust guide and the recommended easy week between blocks instead (3.5, 3.10; COACH-R3 PER-01). |
| CA-17 | Four aerobic sessions vs "never the same colour" | COACH-R2 SES-06 (RIGHT): the 4th aesthetics session is a second easy Orange; every count adjustable by the user | AE A5 '***Never do the same color workout in the same week' | The answer wins: Orange repeats for aesthetics clients (V21). |

### 7.2 Inconsistencies across the team's files (decisions taken here)

| # | Issue | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| I1 | Size-to-rate mapping: chest/back 5 %, triceps 7 % (03) vs chest/back 3 %, triceps 5 % (06) | Superseded by VOL-05: no size-to-rate mapping at all; v1.1: one stairway for every muscle and person (3.3). |
| I2 | Deload depth: 0.85 x W1 (03) vs 0.6 x previous week (06) vs 50-60 % (10b VOL-7) vs 'about half the sets' (PER-05 assumption) | Resolved by PER-05 and COACH-R2 VOL-07: about half of the hardest (last) week's sets, lighter, only between blocks (3.10); 40-60 % stays only for a pre-competition taper. |
| I3 | Release threshold: 0.75 set (03) vs 1.0 set (06; VOL-06 wording put to the coach) | Resolved by VOL-06 (RIGHT): release at 1.0. |
| I4 | Weekly step: +1 set every 2-3 weeks (sheets) vs +2 sets in one week (chat) vs a separate 'coach lane' (previous draft) | Superseded: VOL-06 (whole sets) + UNI-05 (10 % is too much). One set per bank unit; v0's 10 % chunk is retired; on the 12-week stairway 20 -> 21 in week 3 and 22 in week 5 (3.4). |
| I5 | 'Keep the same over the block' vs weekly rotation in BASEBALL / 4T | Resolved by PER-02 / PER-03: block-fixed default; A/B only for variety seekers; zone-constant rotation for athletic accessories stays INFERRED (open question 12). |
| I6 | Skill anchors frozen >= 8 weeks (10b VARI-3) vs BASEBALL rotating front/back/zercher squat in Part C weekly | Freeze kept for skill anchors (SCIENCE), now also backed by PER-02 (exercises fixed for the 4-6-week block); BASEBALL's weekly accessory-drill rotation stays unconfirmed (open question 12). |
| I7 | 'Elevated' = easier (green, RP A82) vs 'Elevated Push ups (for full ROM)' (LIB AD99) | Store `rom_effect` per exercise. |
| I8 | Previous draft cited the JUSTICE LEAGUE squat twins as 'AI46 / AI50 in JL' | Corrected: JL E8 'Front Squats' (Monday) and JL E35 'Low Bar Back Squat' (Thursday); AI46/AI50 are the library cells. |
| I9 | 03 lists front delts as SMALL; CAT-02 leaves front delts unplaced | Front delts: no direct target, indirect credit only, until placed (VOL-05). |
| I10 | 12 v1 priority start at L2: +5 in its §3.3 table and example 5.2 (15 + 5 = 20) vs +4 in its §3.5 text, pseudo-code and JSON `priority.start_add.L2` | This file used +4 (machine-readable value) in Example A; **fixed in 12 v1.1** (+4 at L1-L2, +6 at L3 everywhere). |
| I11 | 12 v1 example 5.3 bases (quads/hamstrings 10, chest 6, back 8) do not follow one rounding of 15 x 0.7 = 10.5 and 15 x 0.5 = 7.5; no rounding rule is written | This file rounds half up (chest/back 8, quads/hamstrings 11) and proposes round-half-up as the rule; **fixed in 12 v1.1** (§3.4: factor results round half up). |
| I12 | v0 11 capped 'exercises per muscle per session <= 2' and '<= 3 per muscle per week' (VP-8) | Retired by VOL-07: 3-4 exercises per muscle on its day; full-body days 2-4 per muscle group (COACH-R2 SES-05). |
| I13 | v0 11 counted athletic sets 0.5 against a lower-body fatigue budget | Retired by VOL-10: athletic work has its own budget (contacts, throws, reps, meters), never mixed with muscle sets. |
| I14 | Olympic lifts by level: 12 v1 (3.13 table) and the first v1 draft of LV-12 read 'L2+ hang power, L3 full lifts'; 05 v1 gives L2-L3 the full lifts and 03 v1 (change 44) already corrected the same split | Follows ATH-10: beginners and beginning intermediates use DB/KB snatches, hex-bar jumps, hang pulls/cleans; intermediate and up (L2-L3) get the full scope (LV-12). **Fixed in 12 v1.1** (§3.13). |
| I15 | 12 v1 3.11 tags the bodybuilder / powerlifter upper-body share '80-90 % bilateral' as COACH UNI-02; the coach gave no upper-body number (UNI-03 repeats the field-athlete answer: 'the same' logic) | This file marks the upper number PROPOSED (3.7). Whether a bodybuilder's upper share also starts near 50/50 in weeks 1-4 is open question 9. **Fixed in 12 v1.1** (§3.11 tags the upper number PROPOSED). |
| I16 | 'Exercises per session': 12 v1 3.10 marks '8' (a full-body day with calves, or a 90-min session) as PROPOSED | VOL-08's assumption, which the coach marked RIGHT, already scales the count by session length (45 min ≈ 5, 60 min ≈ 6-7, 75-90 min ≈ 8-9 exercises), so 8 at 75-90 min is COACH-ANSWER VOL-08. Whether the 2-exercise core block counts inside that number for non-athletes is INFERRED (it counts inside the field-athlete 10-12 in VOL-08 / VOL-17). **Fixed in 12 v1.1** (§3.10 cites VOL-08); COACH-R2 SES-04 then set field athletes at 12 + the 2-exercise core block. |

## 8. Open questions for the coach

> **Status after round 4 (v1.3).** Answered: very short weeks (Q23: the 2-day rule, COACH-R4 R4-SES-03) and ceiling vs step size (Q25: the final sets are reached, COACH-CX CX-01; COACH-R4 R4-PRG-01). Partly: the next block (Q24: the rule is R4-PRG-04; the "blocks completed in a row" input stays PROPOSED). Not asked in round 4: the shoulders side-delt dose (Q21) and the Focus default (Q22). New open questions are in `BLAZE_CAMP_BRAIN.md` §0d.3.

Only questions that are still open or that his answers raise. "NEW" = not yet asked; the rest name the questionnaire item or the v0 question they continue. **v1.1:** questions answered in round 2 keep their number and carry the answer (→), so references stay stable; question 20 is new. **v1.2:** question 20 is answered by round 3; questions 21-24 are new.

1. **Olympic reps for power athletes (NEW)** [NEW (follows PER-09)]. You confirmed 75 → 80 → 85 → 90 % with 4 x 8 → 4 x 6 → 4 x 6 → 4 x 4 for strength-focused plans (PER-09). For a basketball player or sprinter, do the cleans and snatches keep 6-8 reps per set, or should each set stop at 2-5 reps when the bar slows? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 ATH-05, RIGHT):** DB/KB and pull variants keep the 4 x 8 → 4 x 4 ramp; full squat cleans and snatches use 3-5 reps per set with the same weekly reps, with the coach's eye on speed; % only if tested (GEN-06). Power catches at 3-5 is our reading (12 §7 item 16).
2. **Zones: load position or joint position? (NEW)** [NEW (follows CAT-04, CAT-10)]. CAT-04 says front-loaded = foundational and behind the body = advanced, while CAT-10 says the deep squat and overhead are the same external-rotation zone. Where does a deep goblet squat sit: foundational (front-loaded) or an advanced zone (deep squat)? Does the level follow where the load is, or how deep / high the joints go? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 LIB-02, RIGHT):** the level follows the load position, skill and stability (a deep goblet squat is foundational); the zone is a tag.
3. **20 → 22 in one week (NEW)** [NEW (VOL-04 vs UNI-05)]. Your chest example (20 → 22 sets next week) is +10 %, which you also called 'way too much' (UNI-05). Was it an illustration of 'add sets of the same thing', so 20 → 22 over two weeks at 5 % is right, or is a one-off +2 step fine at about 20 sets? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 VOL-01, RIGHT):** never more than 7 % a week, so no one-off +2 at 20 sets; on the stairway 20 → 22 takes until week 5 (3.4).
4. **Which lifts load the spine? (NEW)** [NEW (follows PER-04)]. You squat once and probably deadlift once a week (PER-04). (a) May the squat day and the deadlift day be two different days (two spine-loading days a week), or should both sit on the same day? (b) Do single-leg and staggered-stance DB deadlifts, DB RDLs and hack squats count as loading the spine, or only barbell / trap-bar squats and deadlifts? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 SES-01, SES-02, RIGHT):** two different days; the axial list counts barbell squats, barbell or trap-bar deadlifts and RDLs and good mornings, not single-leg DB work; hack squats are fine on the second day if not sore.
5. **Glutes with 0.25 helper credit (NEW)** [NEW evidence on 12 decision 3]. With helpers at 0.25, squats and lunges give glutes very little credit. (The engine already counts glutes 0.5 on deadlifts, RDLs and other hinges as a PROPOSED exception to your 0.25: is a hinge's glute work 'helper' work to you, or main-mover work?) In a 4-day full-body plan glutes stayed at 13-14 sets against a start of 17, even with two hip-thrust Focus slots (Example A); counted Pelland's way (indirect 0.5) the same plan gives glutes 16-17.5. Should squats and lunges count more for glutes, should the glute target be lower, or should every physique plan carry direct glute work? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 VOL-05):** 0.25 on squats, lunges, deadlifts and RDLs; hip thrusts and bridges 1.0; KB swings 0. Physique plans therefore carry direct glute work (Example A).
6. **10-20 % bilateral in practice (NEW)** [NEW (follows UNI-02, PER-04)]. For a COD athlete at 80-90 % unilateral, the second weekly squat and hinge become single-leg lifts (box pistol, staggered-stance deadlift), so each bilateral pattern appears once a week (Example B). Is that what you want, or should both bilateral patterns stay twice a week at lower sets?
7. **Same-muscle supersets (NEW)** [NEW (follows ORD-05)]. You use supersets to fatigue people more (ORD-05). For advanced clients, may a superset pair two exercises for the same muscle (e.g. bench + fly), or always a lower + upper pair?
8. **Contra or ipsi by screen? (NEW)** [NEW (follows CAT-13)]. You use contralateral loading to help athletes 'get into the hip' when they lack range (CAT-13). Should the generator choose contralateral vs ipsilateral from a simple hip-range screen, or only by sport (COD → contra, sprint start → ipsi)?
9. **50/50 → 80/20 timing (NEW)** [NEW (follows UNI-02, CAT-13)]. For bodybuilders and powerlifters, should the move from about 50/50 to 80/20-90/10 happen at the block change (week 5), or a little each week? And for the upper body ('the same' logic, UNI-03): does their pressing and pulling also start near 50/50 in weeks 1-4, or stay 80-90 % bilateral from the start (the team's PROPOSED number)?
10. **Grammar-built exercises (re-ask of CAT-12)** [CAT-12 (not understood)]. Can the tool make a new variant by changing one detail of a library exercise (for example 'Reverse Lunges' → 'Reverse Lunges with the dumbbell in the opposite hand'), or should it only use exercises written in your library? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 LIB-01, RIGHT):** library exercises first; one-detail variations between blocks, shown as 'Reverse lunge - dumbbell in opposite hand'; never invented exercises.
11. **Small variables between blocks** [PER-02 (follow-up)]. PER-02 names grip, stance, implement, range of motion and rep range. Are a load-position step (goblet → front squat) and a contra → ipsi swap also 'small variables'? **→ Partly answered (COACH-R2 LIB-01):** "which hand holds the weight" (contra → ipsi) is a small variable; the load-position step stays open.
12. **Athletes' weekly rotation** [v0 Q7 (open)]. BASEBALL changes the exercise in a slot almost every week (about 5 per slot per 8 weeks). Deliberate for athletes ('same speed zone, new drill'), even though PER-02 keeps exercises fixed for a block? Should the main squat, deadlift and Olympic lift stay the same for 8+ weeks?
13. **Mid range of motion** [v0 Q10 (open)]. Does 'mid ranges of motion' mean partials for athletes (1/4 squats, hang positions, box squats) or partial reps at the stretched end for muscle growth?
14. **Tempo** [v0 Q11 (open)]. Is 2-3 s down and fast up your default for hypertrophy? When do you use slow eccentrics: beginners, home clients with light weights, pain?
15. **Stopping a speed set** [v0 Q12 (open)]. 'Same speed for all reps': should a client stop a speed set as soon as the reps slow down, even with reps left?
16. **'Elevated' and '*RDL'** [v0 Q19 (open)]. In 'ROM VARIABLE', does 'Elevated' mean a raised start (shorter range) or raised feet (longer range)? What does '*RDL' mean as the red value in all three variable lists?
17. **Seated presses** [v0 Q21 (narrowed by LIB-13, EQ-04)]. Your zones make overhead advanced and seats easier. Are seated DB presses foundational (as filed) or intermediate? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 LIB-02, RIGHT):** a seated DB shoulder press (and a lat pulldown) is foundational.
18. **KB Face Pulls / Bench Push ups** [LIB-10, LIB-12 (no answer yet)]. 'KB Face Pulls ' sits under VERTICAL PULL (LIB U20): move it to rear delts? 'Bench Push ups': hands on the bench (easier) or feet on the bench (harder)? **→ Answered (LIB-10; COACH-R2 LIB-04, LIB-05, RIGHT):** KB face pulls are shoulder work (side and rear delts, traps) on pull or shoulder days, a rear-delt option and a swap; bench push-ups in both versions (hands on the bench foundational, feet on the bench advanced).
19. **A priority muscle in a full-body plan (NEW)** [NEW (follows VOL-07, VOL-02, VOL-08)]. In Example A (4 full-body days + 2 optional Focus days) chest reached 22 sets by week 3 and could not go higher: both presses were at 5 sets, all four Focus slots at 3, and every training day already had 8 resistance exercises. Its cap is 26. You use 3-4 exercises per muscle on its day (VOL-07). For a priority muscle, may the generator swap one non-priority exercise on that day (for example the vertical push on the chest day) for a second chest exercise, or should the client move to a split with a chest day? **→ Answered (COACH-R2 SES-05, PARTLY):** full-body days keep 2-4 exercises per muscle group, "so it might be a little longer day"; in the recomputed Example A chest has two exercises on each chest day and climbs 19 → 23.
20. **R2-SES-05 for field athletes (NEW)** [NEW (follows R2-SES-05, R2-SES-04, VOL-08)]. On full-body days you want 2-4 exercises per muscle group, never 1. For an off-season field athlete you also said 3-4 resistance exercises after core, plyos and Olympic lifts (VOL-08). Applied together, a day that trains quads, back and shoulders needs 6-7 resistance exercises (Example B's Thursday: 13 listed with core, inside your 12 + 2). Does the 2-4 rule apply to an athlete's resistance block, or may an athlete's day train fewer muscle groups with 2 exercises each? **→ Answered (COACH-R3 SES-01, RIGHT):** 2-4 exercises for each group trained that day (knee + pull day, hip + push day) inside the 12 + 2 core; with his cross pattern that leaves 2 jump / throw stations + 1 Olympic lift, so cross pattern stays the default; the same-pattern athletic template (3-4 jump stations) is for size-focused and high-level athletes.
21. **Side delts inside one shoulders line (NEW, v1.2)** [follows R3-VOL-01]. With group credit an L2 bodybuilder's shoulders line is reached largely through presses, face pulls and rear-delt flies, so direct side-delt work is about 5.5-6 sets a week (Example A). Keep it, or hold a minimum side-delt dose inside the shoulders line (a V-taper bias already adds more)?
22. **Focus default (NEW, v1.2)** [follows R3-SES-01]. You said "an extra set or two or three": is +2 the right default, and should the old +4-6 priority push stay as a coach-only option?
23. **Very short weeks (NEW, v1.2)** [follows R3-SES-02]. With your minimums a 2 × 45-minute week holds one big group (hips) plus the calf work (12 §5.5). Right, or should short weeks train the big movers once a week each at 10 sets?
24. **Rebuilding the next block (NEW, v1.2)** [follows R2-VOL-06, R3-GEN-01]. Nothing is stored, so may the plan ask "how many blocks did you finish in a row?" when the client rebuilds, to apply your 5 % higher start and +1 ceiling?
25. **Ceiling vs step size (NEW, v1.2 verification)** [follows R3-VOL-04]. An intermediate's shoulders or arms start at 12 with a ceiling of 22; at about 3 % then 5 % a week they reach 18 in week 12. Keep the small steps and stop at 18, or make the late steps bigger (up to your 7 %) so they reach 22?

## 9. Method, files and reproducibility

**Read for this v1.2:** `coach_answers_round3.md` in full (authoritative); this file (MD and JSON), `12_volume_algorithm_v1.md` / `.json`, `03_volume_dosing.md` / `03_volume_rules.json` and `05_session_architecture.md` / `05_templates.json` in full (the four pairs were updated together to v1.2).

**New computations for v1.2 (session scratchpad `r3_vol/`, read-only on the repo):**

* `ex11_v12.py` imports 12's v1.2 prototype `vol_engine_v1_2.py` unchanged and computes Examples A and B, the bank table and the chat check → `engine_examples_11_v1_2.json`; `mk_tables_v12.py` renders the tables (`md_tables_v12.json`) and `mk11frag_v12.py` the 3.5 and 3.11-3.13 fragments, so no number was retyped. Checks on both examples: no planned step above 7 %, no week at +10 % or more, 2-4 exercises per trained group and exactly one calf exercise per leg day on every day (0 violations), heavy squat and heavy deadlift on different days.
* `edit11md_v12.py` edited this MD in place from its v1.1 text; `patch11json_v12.py` patched the JSON from its v1.1 version (backups in `r3_vol/backup/`).

**Read for this v1.1:** `coach_answers_round2.md` in full (authoritative); `12_volume_algorithm_v1.md` and `.json` v1.1 in full; this file's v1 (MD and JSON) in full.

**New computations for v1.1 (session scratchpad `r2_vol/`, read-only on the repo):**

* `ex11_v1_1.py` imports 12's v1.1 prototype `vol_engine_v1_1.py` unchanged (md5 61ac996561aa590d49e88492db836733) and computes Examples A and B, the bank table, the chat check and the DB-bench volume-load table → `engine_examples_11_v1_1.json`; `mk_tables.py` renders the tables (`md_tables.json`).
* `edit11md.py` rebuilt this MD from the v1 text with in-place edits (section 3 rewritten; the credit map copied from 12's JSON); `patch11json.py` patched the JSON from its v1 version. The v1 build chain below (`tv11_v1/`) is history: its engine (`vol_engine_v1.py`) and examples are superseded.

**Read for this v1:** `coach_answers_full.md` in full and `coach_answers.md` (first; authoritative); `12_volume_algorithm_v1.md` and `.json` in full (the authoritative engine); `05_session_architecture.md` v1 (axial budget, pairing, generic names); the v0 of this file and its sources (`tv11/data_11.py`, `build_11.py`, `11_template.md`, `engine_sim_v2.py`, `bilat_uni.py`, `variation_audit.py`); `dumps/` for every new cell citation.

**New computations (session scratchpad `tv11_v1/`, read-only on the repo):**

* `engine_sim_v4.py` imports 12's prototype `vol_engine_v1.py` unchanged (md5 7b5bc34d4a2c62cc5766591a6f59e545) and computes the bank table, the chat check, the VOL-07 order demo and Examples A and B → `engine_examples_v4.json`.
* `data_11_v1.py` is an overlay on the v0 data: every v0 entry not overridden is carried over unchanged; every override cites the coach answer behind it.
* `build_11_v1.py` renders this file and `11_training_variables.json` from one source (it also copies 12's credit map and share profiles at build time), so the tables, the JSON and 12 cannot drift.
* `bilat_uni.py` and `variation_audit.py` (v0, OBSERVED computations) are re-used as they were.

No Google Sheet was opened or modified.

## Verification log

**v1.1 (2026-10-08, round 2).**

* **Round-2 answers.** `coach_answers_round2.md` read in full (the round-2 questionnaire plus LIB-10 and LIB-12). Every answer that bears on a training variable was traced to the rules, tables, examples and JSON fields that carry it (a change-log line alone did not count): R2-VOL-01..08 → 3.3-3.6, 3.8, V06, V08; R2-SES-01..06 → V25, AX1, 3.6, 3.8, V07, V09, V12, V18, V21, R10-core; R2-ATH-01..08 → V19, LV-4, LV-11, LV-12, LV-14, QM-7, QM-8, 2.2-2.3, 3.13; R2-LIB-01..08, LIB-10, LIB-12 → 1.1, 1.2, V10, V11, V13, V21, V23, VP-4, VP-10, 4.4, KF-1, CA-1, CA-4; R2-PER-01..07 → 3.3, 3.10; R2-GEN-01, GEN-04, GEN-05, GEN-06 → 3.5, 3.13, V01, V08, LV-8. R2-GEN-02, GEN-03, GEN-07 and GEN-08 (website, health check, sample-plan grading, Spanish) do not bear on training variables and are left to the product files.
* **IDs.** Script check: 45 distinct round-2 IDs (COACH-R2 / R2-; all 45 questions of the file) are cited across the MD and JSON, and all exist in `coach_answers_round2.md`; every verdict word written next to a round-2 ID (RIGHT, PARTLY, NOT RIGHT, no chip, not understood) matches the file.
* **Engine.** Every number in 3.3, 3.4, 3.12 and 3.13 comes from `engine_examples_11_v1_1.json` and `md_tables.json`, computed by `ex11_v1_1.py` with 12's v1.1 prototype imported unchanged (md5 in section 9). The credit map in 3.2 is copied from 12's JSON at build time. Starts, caps, the floor, the priority add, the stairway steps, the check-in table, the deload dose, the session limits and the plyo shares were compared with 12 v1.1 (MD section 3 and JSON) and match.
* **Session rules inside the examples** (script over the engine's slots): the heavy squat and the heavy deadlift sit on different days in every week; the core opener opens every training day; every lower/upper pair is posterior lower + push or knee dominant + pull; every muscle group trained on a full-body day has 2-4 exercises (0 R2-SES-05 violations in A and B); Example B lists at most 13 exercises a day including the core block (≤ 12 + 2) and runs at most 89.6 min; Example A runs at most 90.0 min of its 90; the largest week-over-week rise in total resistance sets is +5.9 % (A) and +6.8 % (B), under the 10 % ceiling; no step above 7 %; non-priority muscles never above 3 % while chest was the priority.

## v1.1 verification log

*History (v1.1). Round 3 retired or settled what this log treats as current for R2-VOL-01 / VOL-02 / VOL-03 / GEN-01 (check-in), R2-VOL-04 (rear delts 4 / 6 / 8), R2-VOL-08 (placement) and R2-PER-06 (end prompt); the "Flags kept" list is closed or carried in the v1.2 verification log at the end of this file.*

**Adversarial check of the round-2 update, 2026-10-08.** One log for the three volume files; the same text is appended to `12_volume_algorithm_v1.md`, `03_volume_dosing.md` and `11_training_variables.md`, and a short JSON twin sits in each JSON (`verification.v1_1_adversarial` in 12, `verification_log.v1_1_adversarial` in 03 and 11).

**Method.** `coach_answers_round2.md` read in full first. Then the rule sections, tables, worked examples, pseudo-code and JSON of 12, 03 and 11 (v1.1). Every round-2 answer that touches volume was traced to the rule that carries it in each file, in the Markdown **and** the JSON (a change-log line alone did not count). Leftover scans (regex over all six files, history sections and "retired" notes excluded, every remaining hit read by hand) for: acclimation caps / start-hard 8-week / target-seeking; early or triggered deloads inside a block; sleep, stress, life or physical-job inputs; effort 10 = fail; KB-swing glute credit; 1 exercise per muscle on full-body days; an Olympic once-a-week cap; mid-block tests; 1RM tests for untrained people; mandatory videos. A citation count of every COACH-R2 ID per file was used to find thin spots. All three JSON files parse; their indent (1) and character encoding are unchanged. Backups of the six files before this pass: session scratchpad `adv/backup/`. No Google Sheet was opened or changed.

**Fixes made in this pass.**
1. **KB swings still credited in the observed program views (03; R2-VOL-05, VOL-10).** The decode files bucket KB swings as bilateral hip dominant (his library files them there, OBSERVED), so the v1.1 recompute still gave them hamstrings 1.0 and glutes 0.25. Twelve views changed: MM athletic STARTING (3 swing sets a week), boxer weeks 3-6 (3), 7 (4), 8 (6), baseball block 1 mean (2.29), block 2 mean (3.14), block 2 'WEEK 8' (5), block 2 'DELOAD WEEK' (3), block 3 mean (2). JSON: `normalized_muscles_coach_v1_1` corrected, new per-view field `kb_swing_sets_no_credit_v1_1`, `coach_count_summary.v1_1` medians and band counts recomputed (only glutes and hamstrings move; the other ten muscles reproduce the stored values exactly). Markdown: §3.1 (nine rows), §3.2 athletic row (glutes 6.28 → 6, hamstrings 17.6 → 14.5), §3.3 rows (glutes 25 / 31 / 2 / 0 / 0 / 0; hamstrings 1 / 10 / 20 / 26 / 1 / 0), §0 and the §3.5 / §3.6 sentences (boxer week 8 hamstrings 28 → 22). The `res` column and the §2 / §8 observed totals keep the swings as written (library category); only muscle credit changed.
2. **Next-block start in 12's pseudo-code (MD and JSON; R2-VOL-06, PER-06).** It multiplied the table start by 1.05 after the maintenance and pre-season overrides, so a maintenance phase or a pre-season block after a completed block was inflated by 5 %, and it never compounded block to block. Now: stairway blocks start at the previous start × 1.05 (round half up; 15 → 16 → 17 → 18 as in §3.12 and example 5.4), the same start after a cancelled block (PROPOSED), the table start only for a first block; maintenance and pre-season stay flat with no × 1.05 and no floor.
3. **12 §2.8** still said older clients get "few plyos unless they ask"; now R2-ATH-08 (none by default, optional with the warning).
4. **12 JSON priority caps:** calves were L2 22 / L3 26 (a v1 small-cap leftover) against 12 §3.3's 18 / 26 / 30; rear delts lacked L1 12. Aligned with the Markdown.
5. **12 JSON `supersedes`:** the v1 rows the Markdown marks † (rates from readiness, credit 0.5, deload dose, "8 start hard / 12 acclimation" plan shapes, session budget, readiness gates) carried no marker, so a JSON reader saw retired rules as current. Each now has `changed_again_in_v1_1`. `stairway.retired_v1.phase_limits` now says that only the acclimation and 8-week caps are retired (flat maintenance, flat in-season and fight-prep waves stay live).
6. **12 §7 item 14 (older adults ≤ 3 %):** note added that the R2-VOL-03 proposal he answered "No" listed 60+ among the 3 % defaults; his reason was missing check-in data, not age, so the rule stays PROPOSED for a re-ask.
7. **03 / 11 weight guidance (R2-GEN-06) and Olympic reps (R2-ATH-05):** "% only when a recent tested best is entered, otherwise RIR; Olympic lifts explained as power output" added to 03 §9.6 (strength row), §9.9 (matrix strength and Olympic cells, effort paragraph), the 03 JSON (`progression.by_goal.strength`, `rep_selection.matrix`, `effort_targets`) and 11 §3.3 / JSON `goal_modifiers[1]`; 03 JSON `rep_selection.olympic.L3` "1 to 3 - 3 to 5" → 3-5 reps.
8. **New sports (coach request 2026-10-08):** 12 §3.13 and 03 §9.11 now say how any sport outside his ranked list is placed: by its ground-contact class from `04_athlete_profiles` / `synthesis/sports/` (class 1-5 = 75 / 60 / 50 / 40 / 25 %, his approved shares), e.g. swimming class 4 (25 → 40 %), pickleball, padel and HYROX class 3 (50 %, PROPOSED); 12 §3.10 takes the aerobic count of those sports from their profile (competitive swimmers 0 gym aerobic sessions by default, PROPOSED there). JSON twins: 12 `athletic.plyo_share_by_sport.classes_04`, `session_limits.aerobic_sessions_per_week.other_sports`; 03 `sport_profiles_L2_weekly.other_us_sports_v1_1`.
9. **History and status cleanup:** 03 tension 8, §11.1 #1 / #11 / #12, §11.2 #5 and their JSON twins (`tensions[7]`, `open_questions.answered_v0`, `still_open`) updated to the stairway, short training blocks and between-block deloads; 03 v1 change rows 5, 7, 11, 32, 45 marked † (JSON `changed_again_in_v1_1`); 03 JSON `observed.conditioning.marathon_flag` records R2-LIB-06; 03 JSON `effort_targets.planned_drift_flag_v1_1` and `progression.by_goal_key_note` (the v1 key `person_rate_moves` now means what the stairway moves); 03 §3.1 note names `normalized_muscles_coach_v1_1` as the table source; 11 §8 Q15 and Q23 (and JSON `answered_v0_questions`) and the UNI-05 row (†).

**Adversarial check of the round-3 update, 2026-10-09.** One log for the four volume and session files; the same text is appended to `12_volume_algorithm_v1.md`, `03_volume_dosing.md`, `11_training_variables.md` and `05_session_architecture.md`. JSON twins: `verification.v1_2_adversarial` (12), `verification_log.v1_2_adversarial` (03, 11), `_meta.v1_2_adversarial_verification` (05).

**Method.** `coach_answers_round3.md` read in full first. Then the v1.2 change tables, rule sections, tables, worked examples, pseudo-code and JSON of 12, 03, 11 and 05. Every round-3 answer that touches these files was traced to the rule that carries it in each file, in the Markdown **and** the JSON (a change-log line alone did not count). Leftover scans (regex over all eight files; every hit outside history sections and "retired" notes read by hand) for: check-in; readiness; a weekly step from effort or feedback; `adapt()`; logbook-driven changes and "learn from users' data"; stored user data; rear delts as a line with a floor of their own; Apache / CC BY licensing; a mandatory maintenance or easy week; the old PAR-Q wording. The time policy of the v1.2 prototype (`r3_vol/tvm.py`) was re-run once with one change (fix 6). All four JSON files parse; indent 1, UTF-8 and the missing final newline are unchanged. `node --test tests/` gives the same 7 failures before and after this pass (the stale compiled `model/` and the cross-checks that wait on the engine, flag 3); no new failure. Backups of the eight files before this pass: session scratchpad `r3_adv/backup/`. No Google Sheet was opened or changed.

With λ ≤ 1 a line whose ceiling is more than × G(N) above its start peaks below it: 12-week L2 / L3 shoulders and arms reach 18 / 23 against 22 / 25 (12 §3.7.1 table), while 03 §9.4, 03's v1.2 change row and 11 3.3 said "every trained line reaches its ceiling". Stated as an INFERRED reading in 12 §3.7.1 (JSON `stairway.ceiling_reach_v1_2`) and 11 3.3, wording corrected in 03 and 11, flagged: 12 §7 item 16, 03 tension 33 and question 11.5-5, 11 question 25. Logbook: only as a printed page for the user's own notes ("never read back") and as OBSERVED sheet evidence. Stored data / learning from users: only "nothing is stored" statements and retired notes. Rear delts with their own 4 / 6 / 8 or caps 8 / 12 / 16: only in retired notes, v1.1 history and the observed evidence; every current rule reads them inside the shoulders group. Apache / CC BY / Creative Commons: none in these files. A mandatory maintenance or easy week: none ("recommendation, not mandatory" everywhere). PAR-Q: only in 05 section 9 step 1 (now R3-GEN-01) and the v1.1 05 verification table (history). Level up on '8 - That was perfect' or easier stays: it is the coach's own PER-06 rule (RIGHT), judged by the user or coach at the rebuild, never read from logs.
